Update `CHANGELOG.md [Unreleased]` (toolchain entry, this slug)\n\n#### TDD\n```\nRED:     public_entry_exposes_version_constant() — import { VERSION } from '../src/index.js'; expect(VERSION).toBe(\"0.0.0\") (MUST fail: module absent)\nGREEN:   Create src/index.ts + configs until vitest resolves and passes\nREFACTOR: None expected\nVERIFY:  pnpm test && pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm build\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm build` exits 0 AND `dist/index.js` + `dist/index.d.ts` exist (observable: `test -f`)\n- [ ] `pnpm test` green (2 test files); `pnpm typecheck` 0 errors; `pnpm lint` 0 warnings; `pnpm format:check` clean\n- [ ] Pass: complexity — every file cyclomatic complexity <= 10, verified by `pnpm lint` exit 0 (complexity rule enabled)\n- [ ] Pass: size — `wc -l` on every changed source file reports <= 500 lines\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] All five gate scripts individually exit 0\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` aggregate exits 0\n- [ ] CHANGELOG updated\n\n---\n\n## Phase 1: Theme stub\n\n**Objective:** Flat semantic tokens + `<TheoTUIProvider>` + `useTheoTheme()` with default-theme\nfallback, exported from the public entry.\n\n### T1.1 — Semantic tokens, TheoTUIProvider, useTheoTheme\n\n#### Objective\nImplement `src/theme.tsx`: `TheoTheme` type (flat semantic tokens), `defaultTheme`,\n`ThemeContext`, `<TheoTUIProvider theme?>`, `useTheoTheme()`; export all from `src/index.ts`.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** RED tests for hook fallback (no provider → defaultTheme), provider passthrough\n   (custom theme reaches consumers), and partial-override merge; then implement the minimal\n   context module and wire exports.\n2. **Why it is necessary now:** ChatMessage (Phase 2) consumes tokens via the hook (DIP —\n   Baseline § Architecture boundaries); building the component first would hard-code colors and\n   force a rework. Blueprint ADR D5 locks the flat-token shape; gemini-cli's taxonomy\n   (`semantic-tokens.ts:9-43`) is the naming source.\n\n#### Evidence\n- Context+hook consumption pattern: `knowledge-base/references/ink-ui/source/theme.tsx:63-68`\n  (`useComponentTheme` reads `useContext(ThemeContext)`).\n- Token taxonomy: `knowledge-base/references/gemini-cli/packages/cli/src/ui/themes/semantic-tokens.ts:9-43`\n  (text/status categories).\n- Deliberate M0 simplification (flat, no per-component styles): Blueprint §\"D5\".\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nsrc/theme.test.tsx — (NEW) RED tests (fallback, passthrough, partial override)\nsrc/theme.tsx      — (NEW) TheoTheme type + defaultTheme + provider + hook\nsrc/index.ts       — add exports { TheoTUIProvider, useTheoTheme, defaultTheme, type TheoTheme }\ntests/export-surface.test.ts — extend: new exports asserted\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `src/theme.tsx`: no internal deps (domain layer); depends on `react` only (createContext,\n  useContext) — deliberately NOT on ink (tokens are renderer-agnostic).\n- `src/index.ts`: gains 4 exports; `tests/export-surface.test.ts` and (later)\n  `tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` are the callers.\n- `src/theme.test.tsx`: co-located per `rules/testing.md § 5` (`<name>.test.<ext>` JS/TS convention).\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Data structure (exact fields, per blueprint ADR D5 — role colors + status, nothing more):\n\n```pseudocode\ntype TheoTheme = {\n  role: {\n    user:      { prefix: string; glyph: string; text: string };\n    assistant: { prefix: string; glyph: string; text: string };\n  };\n  status: { error: string; success: string; warning: string };\n};\ndefaultTheme: user glyph \"> \" cyan, assistant glyph \"✦ \" magenta, text undefined-color-safe\nTheoTUIProvider({ theme?: PartialDeep<TheoTheme>, children }) -> merges over defaultTheme (shallow per top-level group — no deepmerge dep at M0)\nuseTheoTheme(): TheoTheme -> useContext(ThemeContext) ?? defaultTheme\n```\n\n- Invariants: `useTheoTheme()` NEVER returns undefined (fallback to defaultTheme — fail-safe\n  default, `rules/error-handling.md § 2` explicit over magic null); merge is shallow at the\n  top-level groups (`role`, `status`) — documented limitation, deepmerge arrives with M6 (YAGNI).\n- Edge cases: provider with `theme={undefined}` === no override; partial override of one role\n  keeps the other role's defaults (test asserts).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. Write the 4 RED tests in `src/theme.test.tsx`\n2. Implement `src/theme.tsx` minimal (parsimony rung 6)\n3. Export from `src/index.ts`; extend export-surface test\n4. expect(frame).toContain(\"◆ \")\nRED:     partial_override_preserves_untouched_token_groups() — override role.user only; expect(theme.status.error).toBe(defaultTheme.status.error); expect(theme.role.assistant).toEqual(defaultTheme.role.assistant)\nRED:     empty_theme_override_yields_default_tokens() — <TheoTUIProvider theme={{}}>; expect(theme).toEqual(defaultTheme) (EC-4: empty-but-valid extreme of the merge)\nGREEN:   Implement theme.tsx until all four pass\nREFACTOR: Extract mergeTheme helper only if the inline merge exceeds ~10 lines (KISS)\nVERIFY:  pnpm vitest run src/theme.test.tsx\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm vitest run src/theme.test.tsx tests/export-surface.test.ts` exits 0 with 4 theme tests + extended export assertions passing\n- [ ] `useTheoTheme` returns a total `TheoTheme` in all cases (no undefined leaks — typecheck proves)\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint` exits 0 with 0 warnings; `wc -l src/theme.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n- [ ] CHANGELOG updated\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: ChatMessage primitive\n\n**Objective:** `ChatMessage` renders user/assistant messages with role glyph + token colors,\nsnapshot-covered including NO_COLOR, integrated through the public entry.\n\n### T2.1 — ChatMessage component (snapshot + semantic tests first)\n\n#### Objective\nImplement `src/chat-message.tsx`: `ChatMessage({ role, children })` rendering\n`<Box>` + glyph prefix `<Text>` + content `<Text>`, colored via `useTheoTheme()`.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** RED tests — snapshot of `lastFrame()` per role, semantic `toContain` assertions,\n   NO_COLOR smoke test, and a shared `renderFrame` helper (one React tick); First production\n  consumer of the theme — proves the DIP seam.\n- `tests/helpers.tsx`: imported by `src/chat-message.test.tsx` and\n  `tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` (T2.2) — single determinism point.\n- `src/index.ts`: gains `ChatMessage`; benchmark + example are downstream callers (Phase 3).\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Signature:\n\n```pseudocode\ntype ChatMessageProps = { role: \"user\" | \"assistant\"; children: ReactNode };\nfunction ChatMessage({ role, children }): \n  tokens = useTheoTheme().role[role]\n  return <Box> <Text color={tokens.prefix}>{tokens.glyph}</Text> <Text color={tokens.text}>{children}</Text> </Box>\n\n# Example\ninput:  <ChatMessage role=\"user\">hello</ChatMessage>  (defaultTheme, FORCE_COLOR=1)\noutput: \"> hello\" with \"> \" cyan-colored (ANSI in snapshot), text default color\n```\n\n- Invariants: role is a closed union — TS exhaustiveness (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess` keeps\n  `theme.role[role]` total); component renders in ONE line for short content (wrapping is Ink's\n  concern, not asserted at M0 beyond the fixed-width snapshot).\n- Edge cases: empty children (renders glyph only — snapshot); NO_COLOR run must contain the same\n  visible text (glyph + content) with zero ANSI escapes (assert `\u001b[` absent).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. CHANGELOG entry (test-only — grouped under the T2 entry)\n\n#### TDD\n```\nRED:     public_entry_composes_provider_and_message_for_both_roles() — import from '../src/index.js'; render provider(custom user glyph)+2 messages; expect(frame).toContain(customGlyph); expect(frame).toContain(\"✦ \"); expect(frame).toContain both contents (RED via intentionally-wrong import first, then GREEN with real path)\nGREEN:   Fix import to ../src/index.js — passes with no production change\nREFACTOR: None expected\nVERIFY:  pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm vitest run tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` exits 0 — imports resolve ONLY via `src/index.js`\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint` exits 0; `wc -l tests/public-api.integration.test.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Benchmark + example (wiring triad closure)\n\n**Objective:** Render-benchmark harness with statistical protocol + committed baseline JSON +\nrunnable example — the milestone's runtime-observability pillar.\n\n### T3.1 — Benchmark harness + committed baseline\n\n#### Objective\n`benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx` (workload) + `benchmarks/run.ts` (runner): renders a\n100-message thread + 300-token streaming append into the last message via ink-testing-library;\ncaptures frames + mean/peak/std-dev ms-per-frame over 1 warmup + ≥ 3 measured runs; writes\n`docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json` and prints a console table.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** Port react-ink's harness pattern to the M0 scale, upgraded with the statistical\n   protocol (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev) and JSON persistence.\n2. **Why now:** The cycle owner requires benchmark DATA as acceptance evidence; ADR D6/D9 lock\n   the harness design + data-only posture. It lands after ChatMessage exists (it renders it) and\n   before CI (which smoke-runs it).\n\n#### Evidence\n- Harness prior art: `knowledge-base/references/assistant-ui/packages/react-ink/benchmarks/run.ts:19`\n  (tsx spawn), `benchmarks/long-thread.bench.tsx:205-230` (frame-time sampling via\n  `performance.now()` + `stdout.frames` deltas), `:272-341` (console reporting).\n- Statistical protocol: `rules/analysis-golden-rule.md § 3` (≥ 3 runs, mean ± std dev, units +\n  methodology + hardware).\n- Artifact requirement: `rules/public-copy.md § 4` (reproducible benchmark artifact under\n  `docs/benchmarks/`).\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nbenchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx — (NEW) workload + sampling + stats\nbenchmarks/run.ts                 — (NEW) discovers *.bench.tsx, spawns via tsx, forwards exit code\ndocs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json — (NEW) committed baseline (generated, then committed)\npackage.json                      — bench + bench:smoke scripts (declared in T0.1, verified here)\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx`: imports `{ ChatMessage, TheoTUIProvider }` from\n  `../src/index.js` (production caller — wiring pillar a) + `ink-testing-library` render.\n- `benchmarks/run.ts`: fs/child_process only; forwards non-zero exit (fail-loud —\n  `rules/error-handling.md § 2`).\n- `docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json`: consumed by future `/analysis` +\n  M1 regression comparisons; `.prettierignore`d (T0.2) so format never rewrites data.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Workload: build 100 `ChatMessage` elements (alternating roles) under one provider; then 300\n  sequential `rerender()` calls appending a token to the last message's text (streaming\n  simulation, no timers — synchronous re-render loop keeps the bench deterministic-ish and fast).\n- Metrics per run: `frames` (stdout.frames.length delta), `mean_ms_per_frame`,\n  `peak_ms_per_frame`; sampled via `performance.now()` around rerender batches — honest\n  heuristic, same as prior art, noted in JSON `methodology` field.\n- Protocol: 1 warmup (discarded) + N=5 measured runs (exceeds the ≥ 3 floor); aggregate\n  `mean ± std_dev` per metric.\n- JSON schema (exact fields): `{ benchmark, date, node_version, hardware: {cpu, cores},\n  workload: {messages, streamed_tokens}, protocol: {warmup_runs, measured_runs},\n  runs: [{frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame}], aggregate: {frames_mean,\n  mean_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}, peak_ms_per_frame: {mean, std_dev}}, methodology }`.\n- `--smoke` flag: 1 measured run, NO file write — CI proof-of-execution (ADR D9).\n- Edge case: if `stdout.frames` yields 0 new frames for a batch (Ink coalescing), the sampler\n  skips the empty delta (prior-art behavior, `long-thread.bench.tsx:210` `if (newFrames <= 0) return`).\n\n#### Pseudo-code / Signatures\n```pseudocode\nasync function runOnce(): RunMetrics\n  instance = render(<App messages=100 />)         -- inside ink-testing-library\n  t0 = performance.now(); framesSeen = instance.stdout.frames.length\n  for token in 1..300:\n    rerender(<App messages=100 streamedTokens=token />)\n    sample(instance, now())                        -- frame-delta sampling\n  return { frames, mean_ms_per_frame, peak_ms_per_frame }\n\nmain: warmup(); runs = repeat(5, runOnce); writeJSON(aggregate(runs)) unless --smoke\n\n# Example aggregate output (real numbers land at implement)\n{ \"mean_ms_per_frame\": { \"mean\": 3.1, \"std_dev\": 0.4 }, ... }\n```\n\n#### Tasks\n1. no shared-state writers.\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm bench` exits 0 and (re)writes the baseline JSON with runs ≥ 3 + mean ± std dev + hardware/node metadata\n- [ ] `pnpm bench --smoke` exits 0 in < 60s (CI-viable)\n- [ ] Baseline committed — `git ls-files docs/benchmarks/m0-chat-message-baseline.json` outputs the path AND `pnpm vitest run tests/bench-baseline.test.ts` exits 0\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint benchmarks` exits 0; `wc -l benchmarks/chat-message.bench.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0; baseline data present with REAL measured numbers\n- [ ] CHANGELOG updated\n\n### T3.2 — Runnable example (demo caller)\n\n#### Objective\n`examples/basic.tsx`: a 10-line program rendering a provider + user/assistant exchange —\nrunnable via `pnpm example`.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** Smallest real consumer program + `example` script (tsx).\n2. **Why now:** Completes wiring pillar (a) with a HUMAN-runnable caller (the benchmark is\n   machine-shaped); it doubles as the TTFATT seed — the snippet a future README will show.\n\n#### Evidence\n- Wiring-triad pillar (a): `rules/cycle-implement.md § Wiring triad`.\n- TTFATT north-star: `ROADMAP.md § Success criteria`.\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\nexamples/basic.tsx — (NEW) provider + 2 messages, ink render()\npackage.json       — \"example\": \"tsx examples/basic.tsx\" (declared T0.1, verified here)\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- Imports from `../src/index.js`; uses ink's `render()` directly (the only file that calls the\n  real TTY renderer). Excluded from coverage (not a test target).\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Must exit cleanly when piped (non-TTY): ink handles it; manual verification step\n  `pnpm example | cat` asserts no crash (roadmap non-TTY constraint smoke).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. CHANGELOG entry\n\n#### TDD\n```\nRED:     Given the example program at examples/basic.tsx When it is run piped (`pnpm example | cat`) Then the output contains both role glyphs and the process exits 0 (behavior covered by T2.2's integration test — the example mirrors it 1:1; no separate unit test for a demo script, per rules/testing.md § 4 \"don't test framework-generated code\")\nVERIFY:  pnpm example | cat  — exits 0, output contains both glyphs\n```\n\n#### Concurrency tests\n(none — single-threaded)\n\n#### Acceptance Criteria\n- [ ] `pnpm example` renders both messages; piped run exits 0 (non-TTY smoke)\n- [ ] Pass: quality — `pnpm lint examples` exits 0; `wc -l examples/basic.tsx` <= 500\n\n#### DoD (Definition of Done)\n- [ ] `pnpm gates` exits 0\n\n---\n\n## Phase 4: CI + project quality-gate enablement\n\n**Objective:** The gate chain runs on GitHub Actions for `develop` pushes/PRs; the kit's\nTypeScript detectors are enabled.\n\n### T4.1 — GitHub Actions workflow + code-quality language enablement\n\n#### Objective\n`.github/workflows/ci.yml` running the five gates + bench smoke on Node 20 and 22;\n`typescript` uncommented in `.claude/rules/code-quality-languages.txt`.\n\n#### Why this step (action + reasoning)\n\n1. **What:** One workflow (pnpm + node matrix [20, 22]) executing\n   `format:check → lint → typecheck → test → build → bench --smoke` as separate steps; enable\n   the TS language gate for `/code-quality`.\n2. **Why now:** M0 DoD requires the gates green IN CI (not just locally); the Node-20 job proves\n   the engines floor (Drawbacks row 2). Language enablement is mandated by\n   `CLAUDE.md § Stack conventions` \"once source lands\".\n\n#### Evidence\n- DoD wording: `ROADMAP.md § M0` (\"all exit 0 in CI\").\n- Engines floor risk: § Drawbacks & Risks row 2.\n- Enablement instruction: `CLAUDE.md § Stack conventions`.\n\n#### Files to edit\n```\n.github/workflows/ci.yml                  — (NEW) gates workflow, node matrix [20.x, 22.x], pnpm cache\n.claude/rules/code-quality-languages.txt  — uncomment `typescript`\n```\n\n#### Deep file dependency analysis\n- `ci.yml`: consumes package.json scripts (T0.1 contract); pnpm version pinned from\n  `packageManager` field (corepack).\n- `code-quality-languages.txt`: read by `/code-quality` detectors + `post-edit-check.sh` hook —\n  enabling it turns on knip/tree-sitter checks for the REVIEW cycle of this very plan.\n\n#### Deep Dives\n- Steps (each its own step for observable exit codes — ADR D7): checkout → setup pnpm/node\n  (cache) → `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` → the five gates → `pnpm bench --smoke`.\n- Trigger: `push` to `develop` + `pull_request` targeting `main` and `develop`.\n- Edge case: snapshots in CI — `vitest run` (no update flag) so drift FAILS the job (fail-fast).\n\n#### Tasks\n1. Write workflow; enable typescript in languages.txt\n2. Push to develop; observe run green (evidence: `gh run list --branch develop --limit 1`)\n3. WebSearch: `<domain-concept> spec <year>` OR `<vendor> canonical <year>`.\n71\t3. WebFetch top result. Extract spec section relevant to agent's question.\n72\t4. Compare to diff's implementation. Read `{REFERENCIA_BLUEPRINTS}` files (if present) + cross-ref with diff. If `{PATTERNS_SKILL_NAME}` is registered, instruct agent to consult that skill before WebSearching.\n\n### Mode B — Vendor canonical refresh\nAgent asks \"is this MCP-spec-conformant?\" or \"does this match Postgres recommended pgvector schema?\". Steps:\n\n1. Identify vendor/spec source. Allowlisted by domain:\n   - `mcp-protocol` → `spec.modelcontextprotocol.io`, `modelcontextprotocol.io`, `docs.anthropic.com`\n   - `pgvector-schema` → `github.com/pgvector/pgvector` (README + docs)\n   - `memory-layer` → `project-a.ai`, `project-b.com`, `arxiv.org` (Generative Agents, MemGPT)\n   - `llm-extraction` → `platform.openai.com`, `docs.anthropic.com`\n   - generic → use discover-web-allowlist.txt\n2. {"parentUuid":"0d45cf3c-2184-4932-9ee6-293fa6a7af06","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa47ddb83133e0f43","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011qKY3h9t2UJH5s4MGz96fe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NzERTpdwpYRG1VhV7pj1Ya","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/paulo/Projetos/usetheo/theokit-tools/theokit-tui/.claude/agents/review-m0-walking-skeleton-2026-07-06/findings/domain-frontend.yml","content":"agent: review-m0-walking-skeleton-domain-frontend\nreview_target: main..HEAD for plan m0-walking-skeleton\ndomain: frontend (TUI — Ink/React terminal component library)\ndomain_specific_patterns_checked:\n  - \"semantic-token DIP (component consumes theme via useTheoTheme, never literal colors)\"\n  - \"provider/context pattern (merge semantics, context value identity, nested-provider behavior)\"\n  - \"React hooks discipline (rules-of-hooks enforcement in lint)\"\n  - \"NO_COLOR / degraded terminal rendering (readable without color — a11y analog)\"\n  - \"terminal width handling (Box width control, long-content wrap)\"\n  - \"color-independent role differentiation (glyph prefixes)\"\n  - \"public API ergonomics (ESM entry, exported types, VERSION constant)\"\n  - \"snapshot determinism (FORCE_COLOR pin + canary + committed .snap)\"\nfindings:\n  - id: F-dom-1\n    severity: MEDIUM\n    file: src/theme.tsx\n    line: 73\n    plan_ref: \"ADR D5 — theme stub: flat semantic tokens via provider; plan Deep Dive locks merge-over-defaultTheme but says nothing about context value identity\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Canonical React provider pattern (context value must be referentially stable); peer evidence: references/ink-ui/source/theme.tsx:53-57 passes a caller-stable `theme` object, never constructs one inline\"\n    summary: \"ThemeContext value is `mergeTheme(theme)` computed inline in JSX — a fresh object identity on EVERY provider render, so every context consumer re-renders even when the theme is unchanged, and React.memo on future primitives is defeated\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```tsx\n      return (\n        <ThemeContext.Provider value={mergeTheme(theme)}>\n          {children}\n        </ThemeContext.Provider>\n      );\n      ```\n      The project's own north-star workload is streaming re-renders (benchmark: 300\n      sequential rerenders under one provider). At M0 with one component the cost is\n      invisible; from M1+ (ChatThread with N messages) every token append re-renders\n      every themed descendant via context, not just via parent-child props.\n    recommended_action: \"`const value = useMemo(() => mergeTheme(theme), [theme]);` — one line, closes the canonical provider anti-pattern before consumers exist. Note honestly: callers passing inline object literals still defeat it, but the default/no-theme and stable-theme paths (the common cases, incl. the benchmark) become stable.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-2\n    severity: MEDIUM\n    file: eslint.config.js\n    line: 8\n    plan_ref: \"ADR D7 — eslint 9 flat + typescript-eslint recommended (no React-specific plugins declared)\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Domain recipe 'frontend-react': verify hooks discipline (no conditional hook calls). eslint-plugin-react-hooks is the only mechanized enforcement of rules-of-hooks\"\n    summary: \"A React component library ships with zero React-aware lint: no eslint-plugin-react-hooks (rules-of-hooks / exhaustive-deps) and no eslint-plugin-react. The codebase already contains the exact shape the rule polices: ChatMessage conditionally throws BEFORE calling useTheoTheme (src/chat-message.tsx:20-25), i.e. the hook is not reached on every code path\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```js\n      export default tseslint.config(\n        { ignores: [...] },\n        eslint.configs.recommended,\n        ...tseslint.configs.recommended,   // <-- no react-hooks plugin anywhere\n        { rules: { complexity: [\"error\", 10] } },\n      );\n      ```\n      ```tsx\n      // src/chat-message.tsx:20-25 — guard-throw before the hook\n      if (role !== \"user\" && role !== \"assistant\") {\n        throw new TypeError(...);\n      }\n      const tokens = useTheoTheme().role[role];\n      ```\n      Today the throw-before-hook is safe in practice (a throwing component never\n      commits, so hook order can't skew between renders), and it is plan-mandated\n      (EC-1 boundary validation). But nothing stops M1+ contributions from adding a\n      genuinely unsafe conditional hook — the gate chain would stay green.\n    recommended_action: \"Add eslint-plugin-react-hooks (recommended config) to eslint.config.js. If it then flags the EC-1 guard as a conditional hook call, resolve by moving the guard AFTER the hook call (useTheoTheme is side-effect-free and total) or by an inline disable with a comment citing plan EC-1 — either keeps fail-fast semantics. Small config change; fits M0 or first M1 task.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-3\n    severity: LOW\n    file: src/theme.tsx\n    line: 49\n    plan_ref: \"ADR D5 + T1.1 Deep Dive — 'merges over defaultTheme (shallow per top-level group)'; nested-provider semantics never specified\"\n    domain_anchor: \"React theming convention (nested providers compose with the ancestor theme); peer check: references/ink-ui/source/theme.tsx:53-61 also does NOT compose (merge is external via extendTheme), so this is a divergence from web-React convention, not from the Ink peer\"\n    summary: \"mergeTheme always merges the override over the static defaultTheme, never over the ancestor provider's theme — a nested <TheoTUIProvider theme={...}> silently DISCARDS all outer-provider overrides. Implementation matches the plan, but the behavior is implicit: undocumented and untested, and D5's consequence ('M0 token names become API') means this semantic hardens too\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```tsx\n      function mergeTheme(override: TheoThemeOverride | undefined): TheoTheme {\n        if (override === undefined) {\n          return defaultTheme;          // <-- base is always defaultTheme,\n        }                               //     never useContext(ThemeContext)\n        return { role: { user: { ...defaultTheme.role.user, ...override.role?.user }, ...\n      ```\n    recommended_action: \"M0-scope: add one sentence to the TheoTUIProvider JSDoc ('nested providers reset to defaultTheme + own override; they do not compose with ancestors') plus one test pinning that behavior. Composing with the parent (read ThemeContext inside the provider) is a deliberate M6 decision — record it, don't ship it silently.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-4\n    severity: LOW\n    file: src/theme.tsx\n    line: 39\n    plan_ref: \"T1.1 Invariants — 'useTheoTheme() NEVER returns undefined (fallback to defaultTheme — fail-safe default)'\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Shared-singleton mutability: mergeTheme spreads FROM defaultTheme, so any consumer mutation of the exported object corrupts every subsequent merge and every unprovided render, library-wide\"\n    summary: \"defaultTheme is exported as a plain mutable object; `defaultTheme.role.user.glyph = 'X'` in consumer code (or a future test) silently rewrites the fail-safe base for the whole process. Types are not Readonly and the object is not frozen\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```tsx\n      export const defaultTheme: TheoTheme = {\n        role: { user: { glyph: \"> \", prefix: \"cyan\", text: undefined }, ... },\n        status: { error: \"red\", success: \"green\", warning: \"yellow\" },\n      };\n      ```\n    recommended_action: \"Deep-freeze the export (three Object.freeze calls or a 5-line freeze helper) and/or type the export as a Readonly structure. Zero runtime cost, protects the invariant the plan calls fail-safe.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-5\n    severity: LOW\n    file: src/index.ts\n    line: 1\n    plan_ref: \"T0.2 — 'src/index.ts exporting VERSION' (placeholder \\\"0.0.0\\\" until T1/T2 add exports); no task ever ties it to package.json\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Public API ergonomics — an exported VERSION that lies is worse than none; cycle-release bumps package.json but nothing bumps this constant\"\n    summary: \"VERSION is hardcoded '0.0.0' and is public API (exported from the composition root), but no test or build step syncs it with package.json — first /release will ship a package whose manifest says 0.1.0 while its runtime constant says 0.0.0\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```ts\n      export const VERSION = \"0.0.0\";\n      ```\n      tests/export-surface.test.ts asserts VERSION exists but (per plan T0.2 TDD)\n      pins it to the literal \"0.0.0\" — the test will hold the drift in place rather\n      than catch it.\n    recommended_action: \"Either extend tests/package-manifest.test.ts (or export-surface test) to assert VERSION === JSON.parse(readFileSync('package.json')).version, or drop the export until a release process needs it (YAGNI). Flag to the release cycle either way.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-6\n    severity: INFO\n    file: src/theme.tsx\n    line: 42\n    plan_ref: \"ADR D4 — glyph prefixes borrowed from gemini-cli (UserMessage.tsx:26 '> ', GeminiMessage.tsx:28 '✦ ')\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Terminal-adaptive rendering: U+2726 (✦ BLACK FOUR POINTED STAR) has East Asian Width = Ambiguous — renders 2 cells wide on ambiguous-wide CJK terminal configs while Ink's string-width counts 1, producing column misalignment; legacy non-UTF8 consoles show mojibake\"\n    summary: \"The default assistant glyph is an EAW-Ambiguous Unicode char; on a minority of terminal configurations alignment/rendering degrades. Honest assessment: gemini-cli ships the same glyph in production, the theme override is a working escape hatch, and M6 owns terminal-adaptive theming — recording so M6 inherits it deliberately\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```tsx\n      assistant: { glyph: \"✦ \", prefix: \"magenta\", text: undefined },\n      ```\n    recommended_action: \"No M0 change. Add to the M6 (terminal-adaptive) backlog: consider an ASCII fallback token set (e.g. '*' / '>') selected when the terminal reports limited capability. I have NOT verified specific terminal emulators' behavior — flagging for a human/M6 spike, not asserting breakage.\"\n\n  - id: F-dom-7\n    severity: INFO\n    file: tests/fixtures/no-color-probe.tsx\n    line: 9\n    plan_ref: \"T2.1 — NO_COLOR smoke test; Coverage Matrix row 8 (NO_COLOR / non-TTY robustness)\"\n    domain_anchor: \"Degraded-output coverage (a11y analog: readable without color)\"\n    summary: \"The NO_COLOR degraded path is proven for exactly one shape: default-theme user role, ChatMessage imported directly from src/chat-message.js (bypassing the public entry and the provider). Assistant role and provider-wrapped composition are never rendered under NO_COLOR. Matches the plan's declared M0 scope ('one NO_COLOR smoke test', full matrix deferred to M6) — recorded so M6 knows the actual coverage floor, not as a violation\"\n    evidence: |\n      ```tsx\n      const instance = render(\n        <ChatMessage role=\"user\">plain text probe</ChatMessage>,\n      );\n      ```\n    recommended_action: \"Optional 2-line hardening now (render both roles under a provider in the same probe, import from ../../src/index.js); otherwise carry as an explicit M6 test-matrix item.\"\n\npositive_observations:\n  - \"Color-independent readability (a11y analog) PASSES: roles are differentiated by glyph ('> ' vs '✦ '), not by color alone; NO_COLOR frame keeps the glyph — verified by the probe assertions (out contains '>' with zero ANSI escapes).\"\n  - \"NO_COLOR test strategy is domain-correct: chalk pins its color level at import time, so the fresh-subprocess probe (execFileSync with a scrubbed env) is the only honest way to test degradation — in-process stubEnv would silently test nothing.\"\n  - \"Snapshot determinism discipline is real: FORCE_COLOR/NO_COLOR/CI pinned at the vitest config root (ADR D2/EC-5), an ANSI-presence canary test guards the pin, snapshots wrapped in <Box width={40}>, committed .snap contains pinned ANSI ([36m/[35m).\"\n  - \"DIP seam holds: ChatMessage carries zero literal colors — all rendering values flow from useTheoTheme(); the hook is total (context default = defaultTheme, never undefined).\"\n  - \"Width handling: glyph <Text> + content <Text> inside a row <Box> yields hanging-indent wrapping for long content in narrow boxes (EC-3 test proves no crash at width=20); the exactOptionalPropertyTypes-safe conditional color spread (chat-message.tsx:29) is the correct Ink idiom.\"\n  - \"examples/basic.tsx is a faithful TTFATT seed: imports only from the public entry, mirrors the T2.2 integration shape 1:1.\"\nverdict_contribution: \"No BLOCKER/HIGH findings. Two MEDIUMs (missing useMemo on context value; missing react-hooks lint in a React component library) are small, well-bounded fixes — compatible with READY_TO_MERGE if batched with other agents' fixes or explicitly accepted with a follow-up task; neither undermines the M0 walking-skeleton claim.\"\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1198,"cache_read_input_tokens":162542,"output_tokens":6223,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1198},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":6223,"cache_read_input_tokens":162542,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1198,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1198,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CckijXf3y3WC6PaPqFcon","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","attributionSkill":"review","type":"assistant","uuid":"24e63bf4-8e89-47af-bb0b-d01d716c0633","timestamp":"2026-07-06T11:26:15.641Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/paulo/Projetos/usetheo/theokit-tools/theokit-tui","sessionId":"56c33c5e-7528-4d27-b50f-010a5e093ccb","version":"2.1.187","gitBranch":"develop"}
